## UC-00829 — Recover after a partial failure

*FORD-4 · Agricultural and veterinary sciences · regime: Physical and engineered systems · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** designers and architects (`BEN-ARCHITECT`) — People making commitments at design stage that others must live with. Scale band: team. Named party in this case: `PARTY-9095`.

**Need and context.** Recover after a partial failure (`NED-RECOVER-AFTER-FAILURE`, purpose class *recovery*). Something stopped part-way and the state is neither before nor after. The setting is a whole building: One structure with a design intent and an operating reality. The people who carry it are designers and architects: People making commitments at design stage that others must live with. What makes this situation particular: the work is interrupted and must resume from a partial state; at least one step cannot be undone once taken; the horizon exceeds the organisation's own memory; a resource needed by several parts is genuinely short; one person or household acts alone, without an organisation.

**Situation.** At the scale of a whole building (`SCL-BUILDING`): One structure with a design intent and an operating reality. The case window is 52 days and the deadline for the last deliverable is day 48.

- Situation tags: **S-INTERRUPTED** — The work is interrupted and must resume from a partial state; **S-IRREVERSIBLE** — At least one step cannot be undone once taken; **S-LONG-HORIZON** — The horizon exceeds the organisation's own memory; **S-SCARCITY** — A resource needed by several parts is genuinely short; **S-SOLO** — One person or household acts alone, without an organisation
- Conditions: `CND-STAFF-HOURS` a fixed pool of qualified staff hours (resource); `CND-STERILE-AREA` a controlled clean or sterile area (environment); `CND-SUPERVISION-ABSENT` the person performing the work is unsupervised (environment); `CND-TWO-KEY` two independent approvals required before an action (authority)
- Time, uncertainty, state and change: `DYN-LONG-HORIZON` a horizon longer than the organisation's memory — Nobody who set this up will still be here; `DYN-PARTIAL-COMPLETION` the work is partially complete when conditions change — The state is neither before nor after; `DYN-SUPPLIER-EXIT` a supplier or service is withdrawn — A dependency announces its own end

**Need and context.** Plan work inside a fixed budget and calendar (`NED-PLAN-UNDER-BUDGET`, purpose class *production*). Everything is possible except all of it. The setting is a court, tribunal or hearing: A forum where the record is the decision. The people who carry it are recruiters and hiring managers: People matching people to work under an obligation not to discriminate. What makes this situation particular: the work is interrupted and must resume from a partial state; steady routine operation with known inputs; a resource needed by several parts is genuinely short; a new arrangement with no operating history to learn from.

## UC-00852 — Measure a condition that cannot be observed directly

*ISIC-T · Activities of households as employers; own-use production · regime: Cellular and organismal biology · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** synthetic and analytical chemists (`BEN-CHEMIST`) — People whose results depend on an identifier generated by software. Scale band: team. Named party in this case: `PARTY-5298`.

**Need and context.** Measure a condition that cannot be observed directly (`NED-MEASURE-CONDITION`, purpose class *discovery*). The thing that matters is hidden, so it must be inferred from something measurable. The setting is a single instrument or sensor station: One device whose readings other things depend on. The people who carry it are synthetic and analytical chemists: People whose results depend on an identifier generated by software. What makes this situation particular: several actors act on one object at the same time; at least one step cannot be undone once taken; the evidence is noisy, missing, contested or assumed.

**Situation.** At the scale of a single instrument or sensor station (`SCL-INSTRUMENT`): One device whose readings other things depend on. The case window is 72 days and the deadline for the last deliverable is day 41.

- Situation tags: **S-CONCURRENT** — Several actors act on one object at the same time; **S-IRREVERSIBLE** — At least one step cannot be undone once taken; **S-UNCERTAIN-EVIDENCE** — The evidence is noisy, missing, contested or assumed
- Conditions: `CND-BENCH-TIME` bookable instrument or bench time (resource); `CND-CONSENT-BASED` the subject's consent is the authority and may be withdrawn (authority); `CND-STAFF-HOURS` a fixed pool of qualified staff hours (resource)
- Time, uncertainty, state and change: `DYN-IRREVERSIBLE-STEP` a step that cannot be undone once taken — After this point the options are gone; `DYN-NOISY-MEASUREMENT` measurement noise larger than the effect of interest — The instrument cannot see what the decision needs; `DYN-SMALL-SAMPLE` too few observations for the confidence claimed — The honest interval is wider than the reported one
- Shared resource pools:
    - `P2` staff_hours of a fixed pool of qualified staff hours, capacity **527.7 person-hours** (`CND-STAFF-HOURS`)
    - `P3` bench_time of bookable instrument or bench time, capacity **58.2 bookable slots** (`CND-BENCH-TIME`)

## UC-00127 — Reconcile conflicting records of the same object

*ISIC-J · Information and communication · regime: Ecological and environmental systems · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** communities downstream of an industrial facility (`BEN-DOWNSTREAM-COMM`) — People who bear the consequence of a failure they did not choose. Scale band: community. Named party in this case: `PARTY-3664`.

**Need and context.** Reconcile conflicting records of the same object (`NED-RECONCILE-CLAIMS`, purpose class *accountability*). Two records that should agree do not, and someone must decide what to do about it. The setting is a neighbourhood: Small enough to know, large enough to disagree. The people who carry it are communities downstream of an industrial facility: People who bear the consequence of a failure they did not choose. What makes this situation particular: several actors act on one object at the same time; parties disagree about facts, entitlement or attribution; a resource needed by several parts is genuinely short.

**Situation.** At the scale of a neighbourhood (`SCL-NEIGHBOURHOOD`): Small enough to know, large enough to disagree. The case window is 188 days and the deadline for the last deliverable is day 161.

- Situation tags: **S-CONCURRENT** — Several actors act on one object at the same time; **S-DISPUTE** — Parties disagree about facts, entitlement or attribution; **S-SCARCITY** — A resource needed by several parts is genuinely short

## UC-00001 — Repair rather than replace an object

*ISIC-A · Agriculture, forestry and fishing · regime: Households and everyday life · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** customary and informal land claimants (`BEN-LAND-CLAIMANT`) — People whose relationship to land is real but not written as ownership. Scale band: community. Named party in this case: `PARTY-9459`.

**Need and context.** Repair rather than replace an object (`NED-REPAIR-OBJECT`, purpose class *maintenance*). Repair is possible in principle and blocked in practice. The setting is a voluntary community group: Commitment without budget, continuity without contracts. The people who carry it are customary and informal land claimants: People whose relationship to land is real but not written as ownership. What makes this situation particular: at least one step cannot be undone once taken; the party who must act lacks tooling, staff or expertise; work happens far from support, often without connectivity; steady routine operation with known inputs; the evidence is noisy, missing, contested or assumed.

**Situation.** At the scale of a voluntary community group (`SCL-COMMUNITY-GROUP`): Commitment without budget, continuity without contracts. The case window is 94 days and the deadline for the last deliverable is day 54.

- Situation tags: **S-IRREVERSIBLE** — At least one step cannot be undone once taken; **S-LOW-CAPACITY** — The party who must act lacks tooling, staff or expertise; **S-REMOTE** — Work happens far from support, often without connectivity; **S-ROUTINE** — Steady routine operation with known inputs; **S-UNCERTAIN-EVIDENCE** — The evidence is noisy, missing, contested or assumed
- Conditions: `CND-REMOTE-LOCATION` long travel times to the site (environment); `CND-SINGLE-OWNER` one accountable owner with full authority (authority); `CND-SPARE-PARTS-STOCK` a limited spare-parts stock (resource); `CND-STAFF-HOURS` a fixed pool of qualified staff hours (resource); `CND-SUPERVISION-ABSENT` the person performing the work is unsupervised (environment)
- Time, uncertainty, state and change: `DYN-CONTENTION-PEAK` contention for a shared resource peaks — Everything fits on average and nothing fits at the peak; `DYN-CONTESTED-EVIDENCE` the parties disagree about the evidence itself — There is no neutral record to appeal to; `DYN-SUCCESSION` custody passes to a successor — Someone else will hold this, and must be able to
- Shared resource pools:
    - `P1` spare_parts of a limited spare-parts stock, capacity **107.0 items** (`CND-SPARE-PARTS-STOCK`)
    - `P2` staff_hours of a fixed pool of qualified staff hours, capacity **1621.9 person-hours** (`CND-STAFF-HOURS`)

## UC-00050 — Reach a service one is entitled to

*FORD-5 · Social sciences · regime: Organizations and markets · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** donors and the funding public (`BEN-DONOR-PUBLIC`) — People who paid and want to know what happened. Scale band: community. Named party in this case: `PARTY-5510`.

**Need and context.** Reach a service one is entitled to (`NED-REACH-SERVICE`, purpose class *access*). The entitlement exists; the path to it does not. The setting is a public agency: A body that must act only as its powers allow. The people who carry it are donors and the funding public: People who paid and want to know what happened. What makes this situation particular: approval sits with one body and delivery with another; members of the public encounter the result directly; steady routine operation with known inputs; the evidence is noisy, missing, contested or assumed.

**Situation.** At the scale of a public agency (`SCL-AGENCY`): A body that must act only as its powers allow. The case window is 85 days and the deadline for the last deliverable is day 46.

- Situation tags: **S-DIVIDED-AUTHORITY** — Approval sits with one body and delivery with another; **S-PUBLIC-FACING** — Members of the public encounter the result directly; **S-ROUTINE** — Steady routine operation with known inputs; **S-UNCERTAIN-EVIDENCE** — The evidence is noisy, missing, contested or assumed
- Conditions: `CND-COMMITTEE-CONSENSUS` a committee that must reach consensus (authority); `CND-OPEN-PUBLICATION` an expectation that results are published openly (environment); `CND-STAFF-HOURS` a fixed pool of qualified staff hours (resource); `CND-VENUE-CAPACITY` a venue with a fixed capacity and licence (resource)
- Time, uncertainty, state and change: `DYN-EMBARGO` a period during which a result may not be released — The result exists and may not yet be said; `DYN-MISSING-DATA` systematically missing data — What is missing is not missing at random; `DYN-STALE-STATE` state that expires and must be refreshed — Old truth presented as current truth
- Shared resource pools:
    - `P1` staff_hours of a fixed pool of qualified staff hours, capacity **2921.1 person-hours** (`CND-STAFF-HOURS`)
    - `P2` venue of a venue with a fixed capacity and licence, capacity **1849.5 items** (`CND-VENUE-CAPACITY`)

**Need and context.** Allocate a scarce shared facility fairly (`NED-SCHEDULE-SCARCE`, purpose class *coordination*). Demand exceeds the facility, and the allocation itself must be defensible. The setting is a neighbourhood: Small enough to know, large enough to disagree. The people who carry it are small organisations without specialist staff: Small bodies that must satisfy requirements written for large ones. What makes this situation particular: independent parties must act together to get any result; several actors act on one object at the same time; several working languages must carry the same obligations; steady routine operation with known inputs.

**Situation.** At the scale of a neighbourhood (`SCL-NEIGHBOURHOOD`): Small enough to know, large enough to disagree. The case window is 56 days and the deadline for the last deliverable is day 47.

## UC-00015 — Keep a record usable after its software and its authors are gone

*FORD-3 · Medical and health sciences · regime: Households and everyday life · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** communities downstream of an industrial facility (`BEN-DOWNSTREAM-COMM`) — People who bear the consequence of a failure they did not choose. Scale band: community. Named party in this case: `PARTY-7530`.

**Need and context.** Keep a record usable after its software and its authors are gone (`NED-PRESERVE-RECORD`, purpose class *preservation*). Preservation is a promise about a time when nobody is left to fix it. The setting is a neighbourhood: Small enough to know, large enough to disagree. The people who carry it are communities downstream of an industrial facility: People who bear the consequence of a failure they did not choose. What makes this situation particular: the horizon exceeds the organisation's own memory; disclosure could harm an identifiable person; steady routine operation with known inputs; a foreseeable failure injures people or destroys value.

**Situation.** At the scale of a neighbourhood (`SCL-NEIGHBOURHOOD`): Small enough to know, large enough to disagree. The case window is 32 days and the deadline for the last deliverable is day 10.

## UC-00045 — Reduce exposure of people to a known hazard

*ISIC-H · Transportation and storage · regime: Cellular and organismal biology · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** unpaid family caregivers (`BEN-CAREGIVER`) — Relatives providing care with no training and no relief. Scale band: household. Named party in this case: `PARTY-8927`.

**Need and context.** Reduce exposure of people to a known hazard (`NED-REDUCE-EXPOSURE`, purpose class *safety*). The hazard cannot be removed, so exposure is the variable. The setting is a single instrument or sensor station: One device whose readings other things depend on. The people who carry it are unpaid family caregivers: Relatives providing care with no training and no relief. What makes this situation particular: several actors act on one object at the same time; at least one step cannot be undone once taken; the horizon exceeds the organisation's own memory; a short window governs when the work is possible at all; the evidence is noisy, missing, contested or assumed.

**Situation.** At the scale of a single instrument or sensor station (`SCL-INSTRUMENT`): One device whose readings other things depend on. The case window is 106 days and the deadline for the last deliverable is day 97.

- Situation tags: **S-CONCURRENT** — Several actors act on one object at the same time; **S-IRREVERSIBLE** — At least one step cannot be undone once taken; **S-LONG-HORIZON** — The horizon exceeds the organisation's own memory; **S-SEASONAL** — A short window governs when the work is possible at all; **S-UNCERTAIN-EVIDENCE** — The evidence is noisy, missing, contested or assumed
- Conditions: `CND-BENCH-TIME` bookable instrument or bench time (resource); `CND-CONSENT-BASED` the subject's consent is the authority and may be withdrawn (authority); `CND-HEAT-HUMIDITY` heat and humidity affecting material and people (environment); `CND-SEASONAL-PEAK` a strong seasonal peak in demand (environment); `CND-STAFF-HOURS` a fixed pool of qualified staff hours (resource)
- Time, uncertainty, state and change: `DYN-CADENCE-WEEKLY` a weekly cadence — A rhythm slow enough to plan and fast enough to correct; `DYN-IRREVERSIBLE-STEP` a step that cannot be undone once taken — After this point the options are gone; `DYN-MODEL-UNCERTAINTY` the model structure itself is uncertain — Two defensible structures give different answers

## UC-00382 — Mark an occasion or a loss in a way that holds

*FORD-6 · Humanities and the arts · regime: Households and everyday life · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** family historians (`BEN-GENEALOGIST`) — People reconstructing kinship from records that disagree. Scale band: individual. Named party in this case: `PARTY-7063`.

**Need and context.** Mark an occasion or a loss in a way that holds (`NED-COMMEMORATE`, purpose class *expression*). Ritual and record do different work, and both are needed. The setting is a voluntary community group: Commitment without budget, continuity without contracts. The people who carry it are family historians: People reconstructing kinship from records that disagree. What makes this situation particular: approval sits with one body and delivery with another; the horizon exceeds the organisation's own memory; a resource needed by several parts is genuinely short; a short window governs when the work is possible at all.

**Situation.** At the scale of a voluntary community group (`SCL-COMMUNITY-GROUP`): Commitment without budget, continuity without contracts. The case window is 75 days and the deadline for the last deliverable is day 44.

## UC-00640 — Teach a procedure to someone who will perform it unsupervised

*ISIC-K · Financial and insurance activities · regime: Ecological and environmental systems · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** agronomists and farm advisors (`BEN-AGRONOMIST`) — Advisors who translate measurements into a recommendation a grower will act on. Scale band: team. Named party in this case: `PARTY-1697`.

**Need and context.** Teach a procedure to someone who will perform it unsupervised (`NED-TEACH-PROCEDURE`, purpose class *learning*). Instruction has to survive the absence of the instructor. The setting is a neighbourhood: Small enough to know, large enough to disagree. The people who carry it are agronomists and farm advisors: Advisors who translate measurements into a recommendation a grower will act on. ## UC-01459 — Keep care continuous across handovers and interruptions

*ISIC-P · Education · regime: Households and everyday life · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** learners and students (`BEN-LEARNER`) — People whose progress depends on being able to resume after interruption. Scale band: individual. Named party in this case: `PARTY-5543`.

**Need and context.** Keep care continuous across handovers and interruptions (`NED-CARE-CONTINUITY`, purpose class *care*). Care fails at the seams more often than in the middle. The setting is a small or medium enterprise: One organisation without specialist functions. The people who carry it are learners and students: People whose progress depends on being able to resume after interruption. What makes this situation particular: the work is interrupted and must resume from a partial state; demand or contention at its seasonal or daily peak; steady routine operation with known inputs; a short window governs when the work is possible at all.

**Situation.** At the scale of a small or medium enterprise (`SCL-SME`): One organisation without specialist functions. The case window is 60 days and the deadline for the last deliverable is day 36.

- Situation tags: **S-INTERRUPTED** — The work is interrupted and must resume from a partial state; **S-PEAK** — Demand or contention at its seasonal or daily peak; **S-ROUTINE** — Steady routine operation with known inputs; **S-SEASONAL** — A short window governs when the work is possible at all

The decision cannot wait for better evidence, and pretending otherwise is the failure. The setting is a multi-tier supply chain: Parties who know their neighbours and not the chain. The people who carry it are firms subject to an external requirement: Organisations that must prove, not merely assert, that they complied. What makes this situation particular: an external party can compel evidence about what was done; approval sits with one body and delivery with another; the work is interrupted and must resume from a partial state; steady routine operation with known inputs.

## UC-00626 — Carry out a household task safely without professional support

*ISIC-S · Other service activities · regime: Cellular and organismal biology · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** unpaid family caregivers (`BEN-CAREGIVER`) — Relatives providing care with no training and no relief. Scale band: household. Named party in this case: `PARTY-9803`.

**Need and context.** Carry out a household task safely without professional support (`NED-RUN-HOUSEHOLD`, purpose class *everyday_life*). A domestic task with a real failure mode and no professional in the room. The setting is an extended family or care network: People bound by obligation rather than contract. The people who carry it are unpaid family caregivers: Relatives providing care with no training and no relief. What makes this situation particular: several actors act on one object at the same time; the work is interrupted and must resume from a partial state; at least one step cannot be undone once taken; the party who must act lacks tooling, staff or expertise.

**Situation.** At the scale of an extended family or care network (`SCL-EXTENDED-FAMILY`): People bound by obligation rather than contract. The case window is 54 days and the deadline for the last deliverable is day 50.

## UC-00002 — Hand over work at a boundary without losing what is open

*ISIC-Q · Human health and social work activities · regime: Cellular and organismal biology · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** clinical trial teams (`BEN-TRIALIST`) — People bound to an analysis plan written before the data existed. Scale band: organization. Named party in this case: `PARTY-3650`.

**Need and context.** Hand over work at a boundary without losing what is open (`NED-HANDOVER-SHIFT`, purpose class *coordination*). The next person needs the open items, not the finished ones. The setting is an extended family or care network: People bound by obligation rather than contract. The people who carry it are clinical trial teams: People bound to an analysis plan written before the data existed. What makes this situation particular: the work is interrupted and must resume from a partial state; the horizon exceeds the organisation's own memory; demand or contention at its seasonal or daily peak; disclosure could harm an identifiable person.

## UC-00005 — Get a representative picture of a population

*ISIC-B · Mining and quarrying · regime: Organizations and markets · generation genv-2.1.0, dictionary 2.1.0*

**Beneficiary.** small retailers and market traders (`BEN-RETAILER`) — Traders handling goods, prices and receipts with no back office. Scale band: organization. Named party in this case: `PARTY-3690`.

**Need and context.** Get a representative picture of a population (`NED-SAMPLE-POPULATION`, purpose class *discovery*). Everything downstream depends on who was and was not asked. The setting is a market of many buyers and sellers: No party can instruct another and all of them respond to price. The people who carry it are small retailers and market traders: Traders handling goods, prices and receipts with no back office. What makes this situation particular: several actors act on one object at the same time; work happens far from support, often without connectivity; one person or household acts alone, without an organisation.

**Situation.** At the scale of a market of many buyers and sellers (`SCL-MARKET`): No party can instruct another and all of them respond to price. The case window is 66 days and the deadline for the last deliverable is day 37.

- Situation tags: **S-CONCURRENT** — Several actors act on one object at the same time; **S-REMOTE** — Work happens far from support, often without connectivity; **S-SOLO** — One person or household acts alone, without an organisation
- Conditions: `CND-NIGHT-SHIFT` work at night with reduced supervision (environment); `CND-SINGLE-OWNER` one accountable owner with full authority (authority); `CND-STAFF-HOURS` a fixed pool of qualified staff hours (resource); `CND-SUPERVISION-ABSENT` the person performing the work is unsupervised (environment); `CND-VEHICLE-HOURS` limited vehicle or vessel hours (resource)
- Time, uncertainty, state and change: `DYN-CONCURRENT-ACTORS` several actors act on one object at once — Duty and effect attach per actor per moment; `DYN-DEMAND-SURGE` an unplanned surge in demand — Everyone arrives at once for a reason nobody predicted; `DYN-QUEUE-PRIORITY` a queue whose order is itself contested — Who goes first is the decision, not a detail
- Shared resource pools:
    - `P1` staff_hours of a fixed pool of qualified staff hours, capacity **1017.2 person-hours** (`CND-STAFF-HOURS`)
    - `P2` vehicle_hours of limited vehicle or vessel hours, capacity **582.8 hours** (`CND-VEHICLE-HOURS`)

- **`L4` C2 → C3** (`RK-CONSTRAIN`, one part sets a limit the other must respect) *Transfers:* an operating envelope published by C2 *Enables:* C3 can act without waiting for permission each time *Changes:* the admissible operating range of C3 narrows *Shares:* the limit value *At the limit:* on reaching the limit C3 holds at the last admissible value and raises the condition rather than continuing *Limit:* 698.8 person-hours
- **`L5` C3 → C4** (`RK-CONSTRAIN`, one part sets a limit the other must respect) *Transfers:* an operating envelope published by C3 *Enables:* C4 can act without waiting for permission each time *Changes:* the admissible operating range of C4 narrows *Shares:* the limit value *At the limit:* on reaching the limit C4 holds at the last admissible value and raises the condition rather than continuing *Limit:* 285.5 person-hours
- **`L6` C3 → C2** (`RK-COND`, conditional decision gate) *Transfers:* a verdict and the values it was computed from *Enables:* C2 runs only when the evidence supports it *Changes:* C2 becomes conditional rather than unconditional *Predicate:* proportion of a settlement mechanism with compensating actions records passing validation at C3 is at least 0.56 *Otherwise:* C2 is not started; the shortfall is recorded against C3 with its reason

### Assumptions and provenance

- Capacity of kind budget is assumed available in the setting and is not metered by this case; no pool is declared for it.
- Every entity identifier, quantity and date in this record is a hypothetical fixture minted by the generator; none refers to a real party, site or dataset.
- Mathematical consistency of the allocation and the schedule under these hypothetical values does not establish real-world suitability.
- *Open question:* Whether retail banking and payments customers is the right party to carry the obligation created by two independent approvals required before an action.
